<span class="articleInfo pubtime"><em><span id="tmread_11571804141836">1 min read</span></em> <strong>.</strong> <span data-epoctime="2019-10-23T10:29:21+05:30" data-expandedtime="Updated: 23 Oct 2019, 10:29 AM IST" data-sectionname="Markets" data-updatedlongtime="10:29 AM IST" data-updatedtime="2019-10-23T10:29:21+05:30" id="tBox_11571804141836">10:29 AM IST</span>

<meta content="https://www.livemint.com/market/mark-to-market/aggressive-sale-of-low-end-products-adds-colour-to-asian-paints-volume-growth-11571804141836.html" itemprop="url"/>
<meta content="Aggressive sale of low-end products adds colour to Asian Paints volume growth" itemprop="name"/><aside class="fr" id="readMore_11571804141836"><a class="readmore" data-vars-storyid="11571804141836" onclick="getData('11571804141836');" style="cursor:pointer;">Read Full Story</a> </aside></div></article><div class="contentSec"><span id="body_11571804141836" style="display: none;"><aside class="moreAbout">Topics <div class="clearfix">
<a href="/topic/asian-paints">Asian Paints</a>
</div></aside><div>
<div class="mainArea" id="mainArea">
<p>
<strong>Mumbai:</strong> Market leader <a href="https://www.livemint.com/market/mark-to-market/double-digit-volume-growth-of-decorative-paint-firms-an-optical-illusion-1567530021151.html" target="_blank">Asian Paints</a> Ltd's decorative segment registered a double-digit volume growth in the September quarter. But, the growth in the reporting quarter was lower than 16-17% growth in the June quarter.</p><p>According to analysts, volumes grew 13-14% in the September quarter mostly driven by low-end products, including distemper and putty.</p><p>"Even as domestic decoratives business reported double-digit volume growth, the core paints category rose just ~3% in our view," research house Morgan Stanley said in a report on 22 October.</p><p>Sharing a similar view, UBS Securities Pvt Ltd said, "We believe that the company has allowed mix dilution of 5-6% by pushing low-end putty throughput from newly added distributors."</p><p>As anticipated, benign raw material costs aided the company's gross margins which expanded more than 250 basis points (bps) year-on-year in the September quarter. One basis point is one hundredth of a percentage point. Operating margin, however, rose only 60 bps, hit by higher staff cost and other expenditures.</p><p>The paint maker’s consolidated profit rose 67.5% y-o-y to  <span class="webrupee">₹</span>823.40 crore in the September quarter, aided by deferred tax reversal of around  <span class="webrupee">₹</span>149.7 crore. Adjusting for that, the company’s net profit grew 29% y-o-y, analysts said.</p><p>Shares of the company opened Wednesday’s session in the green, up about 1% at  <span class="webrupee">₹</span>1,783 on the NSE. However, the stock’s price has come off from its 52-week high of  <span class="webrupee">₹</span>1,825 it had touched ahead of its earnings on Tuesday.</p><p>On the valuations front, the company remains an expensive bet in the consumer discretionary basket. The stock is trading at a one-year forward price-to-earnings multiple of around 60 times.</p>

<span class="articleInfo pubtime"><em><span id="tmread_11571772220746">3 min read</span></em> <strong>.</strong> <span data-epoctime="2019-10-23T08:35:13+05:30" data-expandedtime="Updated: 23 Oct 2019, 08:35 AM IST" data-sectionname="Companies" data-updatedlongtime="08:35 AM IST" data-updatedtime="2019-10-23T08:35:13+05:30" id="tBox_11571772220746">08:35 AM IST</span>

<span class="articleInfo pubtime"><em><span id="tmread_11571767535025">3 min read</span></em> <strong>.</strong> <span data-epoctime="2019-10-22T23:48:48+05:30" data-expandedtime="Updated: 22 Oct 2019, 11:48 PM IST" data-sectionname="Companies" data-updatedlongtime="22 Oct 2019" data-updatedtime="2019-10-22T23:48:48+05:30" id="tBox_11571767535025">22 Oct 2019</span>

<p class="summary"></p><ul class="highlights"><li>The draft forensic audit report will be presented to the board once it is finalized</li><li>Out of the around  <span class="webrupee">₹</span>27,000 cr worth of loans raised by DHFL from banks for on-lending to home buyers, around  <span class="webrupee">₹</span>10,050 cr was invested in mutual funds, findings of the audit revealed</li></ul><p></p></div><div class="clearfix socialHolder Gplus" id="socialHolder">

<span class="articleInfo pubtime"><em><span id="tmread_11571746078609">6 min read</span></em> <strong>.</strong> <span data-epoctime="2019-10-23T00:19:22+05:30" data-expandedtime="Updated: 23 Oct 2019, 12:19 AM IST" data-sectionname="Insurance" data-updatedlongtime="12:19 AM IST" data-updatedtime="2019-10-23T00:19:22+05:30" id="tBox_11571746078609">12:19 AM IST</span>

<p class="summary"></p><ul class="highlights"><li>Policyholders are at the receiving end due to high surrender costs and poor disclosure about returns</li><li>Due to the high surrender penalties, early exit from traditional plans means loss of the principal invested</li></ul><p></p></div><div class="clearfix socialHolder Gplus" id="socialHolder">

<span class="articleInfo pubtime"><em><span id="tmread_11571738465907">9 min read</span></em> <strong>.</strong> <span data-epoctime="2019-10-22T23:42:15+05:30" data-expandedtime="Updated: 22 Oct 2019, 11:42 PM IST" data-sectionname="Money" data-updatedlongtime="22 Oct 2019" data-updatedtime="2019-10-22T23:42:15+05:30" id="tBox_11571738465907">22 Oct 2019</span>
</span><span class="articleInfo author">
<a href="/Search/Link/Author/Neil Borate"><strong>Neil Borate</strong></a>
</span>
<p class="summary"></p><ul class="highlights"><li>SIP stoppages have grown by around 4% in the past six months, according to industry data</li><li>Stopping SIPs is a poor strategy for an equity investor because markets give asymmetric returns over different periods</li></ul><p></p></div><div class="clearfix socialHolder Gplus" id="socialHolder">

<span class="articleInfo pubtime"><em><span id="tmread_11571761878443">9 min read</span></em> <strong>.</strong> <span data-epoctime="2019-10-22T22:19:29+05:30" data-expandedtime="Updated: 22 Oct 2019, 10:19 PM IST" data-sectionname="Companies" data-updatedlongtime="22 Oct 2019" data-updatedtime="2019-10-22T22:19:29+05:30" id="tBox_11571761878443">22 Oct 2019</span>
</span><span class="articleInfo author">
<a href="/Search/Link/Author/Mihir Dalal"><strong>Mihir Dalal</strong></a>, <a href="/Search/Link/Author/Varsha Bansal"><strong>Varsha Bansal</strong></a>
</span>
<p class="summary"></p><ul class="highlights"><li>Founders of Indian startups are looking at ways to take back control from investors. What are the implications?</li><li>Founders’ attempts are driven by their need to ensure they don’t lose control of their firms since, at most Indian unicorns, investors control an overwhelming majority of shares</li></ul><p></p></div><div class="clearfix socialHolder Gplus" id="socialHolder">

Photo: iStock</div><div class="linkclick-candidate" data-vars-storyid="11571769041424" onclick="getData('11571769041424');"> <h2 class="headline" data-mob="What equity-linked debentures are and how to use them" data-reg="What equity-linked debentures are and how to use them" data-title="What equity-linked debentures are and how to use them" id="headline_11571769041424"><a href="https://www.livemint.com/money/personal-finance/what-equity-linked-debentures-are-and-how-to-use-them-11571769041424.html" onclick="event.preventDefault();">What equity-linked debentures are and how to use them</a></h2>
<span class="articleInfo pubtime"><em><span id="tmread_11571769041424">1 min read</span></em> <strong>.</strong> <span data-epoctime="2019-10-23T00:10:23+05:30" data-expandedtime="Updated: 23 Oct 2019, 12:10 AM IST" data-sectionname="Money" data-updatedlongtime="12:10 AM IST" data-updatedtime="2019-10-23T00:10:23+05:30" id="tBox_11571769041424">12:10 AM IST</span>
</span><span class="articleInfo author">
<a href="/Search/Link/Author/Sunita Abraham"><strong>Sunita Abraham</strong></a>
</span>
<p class="summary">ELDs are structured fixed-income products that combine the benefits of capital protection along with participation in returns from equity markets</p></div><div class="clearfix socialHolder Gplus" id="socialHolder">

<span class="articleInfo pubtime"><em><span id="tmread_11571770345191">2 min read</span></em> <strong>.</strong> <span data-epoctime="2019-10-23T00:36:54+05:30" data-expandedtime="Updated: 23 Oct 2019, 12:36 AM IST" data-sectionname="News" data-updatedlongtime="12:36 AM IST" data-updatedtime="2019-10-23T00:36:54+05:30" id="tBox_11571770345191">12:36 AM IST</span>

<strong>Julie Verhage</strong>

 <strong>Kurt Wagner</strong>
<strong>, Bloomberg </strong>

<p class="summary"></p><ul class="highlights"><li>Zuckerberg said Facebook is seeking to offer an affordable solution to existing methods of sending money abroad</li><li>The Libra Association was officially created last week, which means Facebook is now just one of 21 members, each with the same voting control over important decisions</li></ul><p></p></div><div class="clearfix socialHolder Gplus" id="socialHolder">

<span class="articleInfo pubtime"><em><span id="tmread_11571743836867">1 min read</span></em> <strong>.</strong> <span data-epoctime="2019-10-23T00:01:19+05:30" data-expandedtime="Updated: 23 Oct 2019, 12:01 AM IST" data-sectionname="News" data-updatedlongtime="12:01 AM IST" data-updatedtime="2019-10-23T00:01:19+05:30" id="tBox_11571743836867">12:01 AM IST</span>

<span class="articleInfo pubtime"><em><span id="tmread_11571801806652">1 min read</span></em> <strong>.</strong> <span data-epoctime="2019-10-23T10:25:11+05:30" data-expandedtime="Updated: 23 Oct 2019, 10:25 AM IST" data-sectionname="Markets" data-updatedlongtime="10:25 AM IST" data-updatedtime="2019-10-23T10:25:11+05:30" id="tBox_11571801806652">10:25 AM IST</span>

 Edited By <a href="/Search/Link/Author/Surajit Dasgupta"><strong>Surajit Dasgupta</strong></a>
</span>
<p class="summary"></p><ul class="highlights"><li>Gold prices in India have remained volatile over the past few days</li><li>In India, gold rates have corrected by around  <span class="webrupee">₹</span>2,000 per 10 gram from last month's highs</li></ul><p></p></div><div class="clearfix socialHolder Gplus" id="socialHolder">

<span class="articleInfo pubtime"><em><span id="tmread_11571750137219">2 min read</span></em> <strong>.</strong> <span data-epoctime="2019-10-22T18:57:11+05:30" data-expandedtime="Updated: 22 Oct 2019, 06:57 PM IST" data-sectionname="Industry" data-updatedlongtime="22 Oct 2019" data-updatedtime="2019-10-22T18:57:11+05:30" id="tBox_11571750137219">22 Oct 2019</span>
</span> <span class="articleInfo author">
 IANS
 </span>
<p class="summary"></p><ul class="highlights"><li>Fear of a CVC probe in case of loan defaults has paralysed bankers and the banking system and this power should be taken away, says the Nobel laureate</li><li>He said such crises are happening repeatedly and if there is a problem in bank balance sheets, then it should be stopped way ahead before it explodes</li></ul><p></p></div><div class="clearfix socialHolder Gplus" id="socialHolder">

<script>

function subscribe(url,params) {
var xhttp = new XMLHttpRequest();
xhttp.onreadystatechange = function() {
	if (this.readyState == 4 && this.status == 200) {
			var rt = JSON.parse(this.responseText);
			if(rt.data.code == "SUCCESS") {
			   if(typeof document.getElementById("subReminderPopUp") !==undefined && document.getElementById("subReminderPopUp")!=null){
			  	document.getElementById("subReminderPopUp").style.display="block";
			   }
			  if(typeof document.getElementById("subscriptionBox") !==undefined && document.getElementById("subscriptionBox")!=null){
			  	document.getElementById("subscriptionBox").style.display = "none";
			  }
			  //setCookie("newsletter", "", -1);
			    var date = new Date();
	            date.setTime(date.getTime() + (89 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000));
	            var expires = "; expires=" + date.toGMTString();
	            var domain = "; domain=.livemint.com;path=/";
	            document.cookie = "newsletter=true" + expires + domain;
	            
	            // call newsletter story function to hide subscription box from stories
			             checkNewsletterStory();
			}
	}
};
xhttp.open("POST", url, true);
xhttp.setRequestHeader("Content-type", "application/json");
// xhttp.setRequestHeader("Access-Control-Allow-Origin", "livemint.com");
xhttp.send(JSON.stringify(params));
}

function checkNewsletter() {

       var cookie_token = getCookie("_ht_clientid");
		var token1 = getCookie("token");
		if(cookie_token && token1) {
		
		  if(getCookie("loginflow") == 'false' && getCookie("loginflow")) {
		  
			   if(getCookie("newsletter") == 'true') {
					     if(typeof document.getElementById("subReminderPopUp") !==undefined && document.getElementById("subReminderPopUp")!=null){
					     	document.getElementById("subReminderPopUp").style.display="block";
					     }
					     if(typeof document.getElementById("subscriptionBox") !==undefined && document.getElementById("subscriptionBox")!=null){
			             	document.getElementById("subscriptionBox").style.display="none";
			             }
						 var date = new Date();
			             date.setTime(date.getTime() - (89 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000));
			             var expires = "; expires=" + date.toGMTString();
			             var domain = "; domain=.livemint.com;path=/";
			             document.cookie = "loginflow=0"  + expires + domain;
			             
			             
			  } else if(getCookie("newsletter") == 'false') {
			      if(typeof document.getElementById("subReminderPopUp") !==undefined && document.getElementById("subReminderPopUp")!=null){
			      	document.getElementById("subReminderPopUp").style.display="none";
			      }
			      if(typeof document.getElementById("subscriptionBox") !==undefined && document.getElementById("subscriptionBox")!=null){
			      	document.getElementById("subscriptionBox").style.display="none";
			      }
			  }
		  } else {
		    if(getCookie("newsletter")) {
		      if(getCookie("newsletter") != "true") {
		        if(typeof document.getElementById("subscriptionBox") !==undefined && document.getElementById("subscriptionBox")!=null){
		      		document.getElementById("subscriptionBox").style.display="block";
		      	}
		      } else {
		      	if(typeof document.getElementById("subscriptionBox") !==undefined && document.getElementById("subscriptionBox")!=null){
		      	document.getElementById("subscriptionBox").style.display="none";
		      	}
		      	
		      }
		    } else {
		    	if(typeof document.getElementById("subscriptionBox") !==undefined && document.getElementById("subscriptionBox")!=null){
		      document.getElementById("subscriptionBox").style.display="block";
		      }
		    } 
		  }
		
		} else {
		if(typeof document.getElementById("subscriptionBox") !==undefined && document.getElementById("subscriptionBox")!=null){
			document.getElementById("subscriptionBox").style.display="block";
		}
		
		}

} checkNewsletter();

function getNewsletter() {
    var subscriptionUrl="";
    if(window.location.hostname.toString() == "staging.livemint.com") {
        subscriptionUrl = 'http://10.136.172.234:8080/v1/newsletter/subscribe';
    }
    else if(window.location.hostname.toString() == "pre-prod.livemint.com") {
       subscriptionUrl = 'http://10.136.172.222/v1/newsletter/subscribe';
//        subscriptionUrl = 'https://accounts.hindustantimes.com/v1/newsletter/subscribe';
    }
    else {
        subscriptionUrl = 'https://accounts.hindustantimes.com/v1/newsletter/subscribe';
    }

	if(navigator.onLine) {
		var cookie_token = getCookie("_ht_clientid");
		var token1 = getCookie("token");
		
		if(cookie_token && token1) {
		var data = {
		"token": token1 ,
		"siteId": "LM"
		};
		//alert(token1)
		  subscribe(subscriptionUrl,data);
		} else {
			window.open('https://accounts.hindustantimes.com?newsletter=true','_blank');
		}
	} else {
		internetConnection();
	}
}

checkNewsletterStory();

	window.clearTimeout( isScrollingStoryFullView );
	isScrollingStoryFullView = setTimeout(function() {
		checkNewsletterStory();
	}, 66);
}, false);
